   1. tol.kaḷa.23.
   ------------
   319. pālai
   [celavuṇarttiya talaimakaṟkut tōḻi celavaḻuṅkac colliyatu.]
   maṇivāyk kākkai 1māniṟap peruṅkiḷai
       piṇivīḻ ālat talaṅkuciṉai yēṟik
       koṭuvil eyiṉar kuṟumpiṟ kūkkuṅ
       kaṭuviṉai maṟavar villiṭat tolaintōr
       paṭupiṇaṅ kavarum pāḻpaṭu naṉantalai         5
       aṇaṅkeṉa urutta nōkkiṉ aiyeṉa
       nuṇaṅkiya numpiṉ nuṇkēḻ māmaip
       poṉvī vēṅkaip putumalar 2puraiya
       naṉṉiṟat teḻunta cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamulaic
       curumpār kūntaṟ peruntōḷ ivaḷvayiṟ         10
       pirintaṉir akaṟal cūḻiṉ arumporuḷ
       eytuka mātō numakkē koykuḻait
       3taḷirēr aṉṉa tāṅkarum matukaiyaḷ
       melliyaḷ iḷaiyaṉ naṉipēr aṉpiṉaḷ
       celvēm eṉṉum nummetir         15
       4oḻivēm eṉṉum oṇmaiyō ilaḷē.
               ------ erukkāṭṭūrt tāyamkaṇṇaṉār.

   ----------
   (pāṭam) 1. māṉiṟap. 2. puraiyum.
   3. koykuḻai. 4. oḻiyōm.
   --------------
   (co - ḷ.) 1-5. maṇi vāy kākkai - karumaṇi pōṉṟa vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya
   kākkaiyiṉ, mā niṟa perukiḷai - kariya niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya periya cuṟṟam,
   piṇi vīḻ - piṇippuṟṟa viḻutukaḷaiyuṭaiya, ālattu alaṅku ciṉai ēṟi -
   ālamarattiṉ acaiyum kiḷaikaḷil ēṟiyiruntu, koṭuvil eyiṉar - vaḷainta
   villaiyuṭaiya eyiṉarākiya, kuṟumpiṟku ūkkum - kuṟumpiṉiṉṟum ūkki eḻum,
   kaṭuviṉai maṟavar - koṭuntoḻilaiyuṭaiya maṟavar, villiṭat tolaintōr -
   villāl eytalāl iṟantupaṭṭōruṭaiya, paṭupiṇam kavarum - viḻuntu kiṭakkum
   piṇaṅkaḷaik kavarntu uṇṇum, pāḻpaṭu naṉantalai - pāḻpaṭṭa akaṉṟa
   pālaiyiṭattē,
   6 - 11. aṇaṅku eṉa urutta nōkkiṉ - teyvam eṉṉumāṟu vaṭivukoṇṭa
   kaṇkaḷaiyum, ai eṉa nuṇaṅkiya nucuppiṉ - aḻakitāka melinta
   iṭaiyiṉaiyum, nuṇ kēḻ māmai - nuṇṇiya māmai niṟattiṉaiyum, poṉ vī
   vēṅkaip putumalar puraiya - poṉpōlum pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya vēṅkaiyiṉ putiya
   malarai yoppa, cuṇaṅku aṇi –
   tēmalaiyaṇinta, nal niṟattu eḻunta vaṉamulai - nalla mārpiṉkaṇ eḻunta
   aḻakiya mulaiyiṉaiyum, curumpu ār kūntal - vaṇṭu moykkum kūntalaiyum,
   peruntōḷ - periya tōḷiṉaiyumuṭaiya, ivaḷ vayiṉ pirintaḷir akaṟal cūḻiṉ
   - ivaḷiṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu akaṉṟu pōtalai eṇṇutirāyiṉ,
   11-6. numakku arumporuḷ eytuka - umakku ariyaporuḷ vantuṟuvatāka, koy
   kuḻait taḷirēr aṉṉa - koyyappaṭṭa kuḻaiyākiya aḻakiya taḷiriṉai yotta,
   tāṅkum matukaiyaṉ - piritaṟṟuṉ piṉait tāṅkutaṟkariya valiyiṉaḷum,
   melliyaḷ iḷaiyaḷ - meṉmait taṉmaiyiṉaḷum iḷaiyaḷum, naṉipēr aṉpiṉaḷ -
   numpāl mikap periya aṉpiṉaḷumākiya ivaḷ, celvēm eṉṉum numatu etiril
   niṉṟu, oḻivēm eṉṉum oṇmai - pirintu āṟṟiyiruppēm eṉṟu kūṟum
   aṟivuṭaimai, ilaḷ - illātavaḷ āvaḷ; (eṉ ceyvēṉ.)
   (muṭipu) pāḻpaṭu naṉantalai, ivaḷ vayiṟ pirintaṉīr akaṟal cūḻiṉ numakku
   arumporuḷ eytuka, tāṅkarum matukaiyaṉ, melliyaḷ, iḷaiyaṉ, naṉi
   pēraṉpiṉaḷ ākiya ivaḷ celvēm eṉṉum etir, oḻivēm eṉṉum oṇmai ilaḷ (eṉ
   ceyvēṉ.)
   (vi - rai.) kiḷai - kūṭṭam. kākkai iṉattuṭaṉ kūṭi uṇṇum iyalpiṉatākaliṉ
   kākkai peruṅkiḷai kavarum eṉappaṭṭatu. ālattu - attu : cāriyai.
   eyiṉaratu kuṟumpiṉaik koḷḷutaṟku ūkki eḻum maṟavar eṉṟalumām. kuṟumpu -
   ciṟṟaraṇ. arum poruḷ eytuka eṉṟatu, ataṟku iṭaiyūṟu uṇṭātalum kūṭum
   eṉak kuṟippittavāṟu. tāṅkarum matukaiyaṉ eṉpatu, tāṅkum
   matukaiyillātavaḷ eṉṟapaṭi. iṉi ivaḷ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ tāṅkarum
   matukaiyaḷ, iḷaiyaḷ, aṉpiṉaḷ, oṇmaiyilaḷ eṉpaṉavaṟṟait taṉittaṉi
   payaṉilaiyākki muṭittalumām.
   --------------
   320. neytal
   [pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ vanta talaimakaṉait tōḻi varaivukaṭāyatu.]
   ōṅkutiraip parappiṉ vāṅkuvicaik koḷīit
       timilōṉ tanta kaṭuṅkaṇ vayamīṉ
       taḻaiyaṇi yalkuṟ celvat taṅkaiyar
       viḻavayar maṟukiṉ vilaiyeṉap pakaruṅ
       kāṉalañ ciṟukuṭip peranīrc cērppa         5
       malarēr uṇkaṇen tōḻi evvam
       alarvāy nīṅkanī aruḷāy poyppiṉum
       neṭuṅkaḻi taḻaiiya kuṟuṅkāl aṉṉam
       aṭumpamar ekkar añciṟai uḷarum
       taṭavunilaip puṉṉait tātaṇi peruntuṟai         10
       naṭuṅakayir pōḻnta koṭiñci neṭuntēr
       vaṇṭaṟ pāvai citaiya vantunī
       tōḷputi tuṇṭa ñāṉṟaic
       cūḷum poyyō kaṭalaṟi kariyē.
               ----- maturaik kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalaic cāttaṉār.

   --------
   (co-ḷ.) 1-5. ōṅku tiraip parappiṉ - uyarnta alaikaḷai yuṭaiya kaṭalil,
   vāṅku vicai koḷīi - valaiyiṉai vicaittu iḻutta lāṟ paṟṟi, timilōṉ tanta
   - mīṉ piṭikkum paṭakiṉaṉ āya tamaiyaṉ koṇṭuvanta, kaṭu kaṇ vayamīṉ -
   taṟukaṇmaiyuṭaiya valiya mīṉkaḷai, alkulaiyuṭaiya celvamikka taṅkaimār,
   viḻavu ayar maṟukiṉ - viḻāc ceyyum terukkaḷil, vilai eṉa pakarum - iṉṉa
   vilaiyiṉa eṉṟu kūṟi viṟkum, kāṉal am ciṟu kuṭi-kaṭaṟkaraic cōlai cūḻnta
   cīṟūr kaḷaiyuṭaiya, perunīrc cērppa - kaṭaṟkaraiyaiyuṭaiya talaivaṉē!,
   6-7. malar ēr uṇ kaṇ em tōḻi evvam - malariṉai otta maiyuṇṭa
   kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya em tōḻiyiṉ tuṉpamum, alarvāy - ūrār vāyiṉ alarum, nīṅka
   - nīṅkumpaṭi, nī aruḷāy poypiṉum - nī aruḷ ceyyātu poyttālum,
   8-14. neṭa kaḻi tuḻaiiya kuṟu kāl aṉṉam - nīṇṭa up paṅkaḻiyait tuḻavi
   mīṉ piṭittuṇṇum kuṟiya kāliṉaiyuṭaiya aṉṉam, aṭumpu amar ekkar am ciṟai
   uḷarum - aṭumpaṅkoṭi poruntiya maṇal mēṭṭiliruntu taṉatu aḻakiya
   ciṟakiṉaik kōti ularttum, taṭavunilai puṉṉai tātu aṇi perutuṟai -
   vaḷainta nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya puṉṉai marattiṉ pūṅkoṭi aḻakuceyyum periya
   tuṟaikkaṇ, naṭuṅku ayir pōḻnta koṭiñci neṭutēr - nekiḻum nuṇmaṇalaip
   piḷantu koṇṭu vanta koṭiñciyaiyuṭaiya nīṇṭa tēriṉai, vaṇṭal pāvai
   citaiya vantu-yāṅkaḷ puṉaintu viḷaiyāṭiya vaṇṭaṟpāvai aḻintiṭac
   celuttivantu, nī tōḷ putitu uṇṭa ñāṉṟai - nī ivaḷ tōḷiṉ nalattiṉaip
   putuvatāka nukarnta nāḷil, kaṭal aṟi kari - kaṭalāṉatu aṟiyum cāṉṟāka,
   cūḷum poyyō - nī kūṟiya cūḷum poyyō.
   (muṭipu) cērppa! em tōḻi evvam alar vāy nīṅka nī aruḷāy poyppiṉum,
   neṭuntēr vaṇṭaṟpāvai citaiya vantu nī ivaḷ tōḷ putituṇṭa ñāṉṟaik kūṟiya
   cūḷum poyyō.
   (vi - rai.) valaiyiṉai vicaittu iḻuttalāl eṉa oru colvaruvittu uraikka.
   celvat taṅkaiyar eṉṟamaiyāṉ, timilōṉ tamaiyaṉ āyiṟṟu. vilai eṉappakarum
   - itaṟku itu vilai eṉakkūṟi viṟkum eṉṟu virittu uraittuk koḷka. evvamum
   alarum eṉuma eṇṇummai tokkaṉa. nī aruḷāy poyppiṉum eṉṟatu, nī varaintu
   koṇṭu ivaḷaip piriyātirukkiṉṟilai eṉṟapaṭiyāyiṟṟu. cūḷāvatu iyaṟkaip
   puṇarccik kālattu yāṉ niṉṉai varaintu koṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻttuvēṉ eṉak
   kaṭaṟṟeyvam cāṉṟākak kūṟiya col. cūḷ piḻaittal takutiyaṉṟākaliṉ atu
   piḻaiyātu ivaḷai varaintu koḷka eṉat tōḻi varaivu kaṭāyiṉā ḷāyiṟṟu.
   ----------
   321. pālai
   [makaṭ pōkkiya cevili colliyatu.]
   pacitta yāṉaip paḻaṅkaṇ aṉṉa
       vaṟuñcuṉai mukanta kōṭait teḷviḷi
       vicittuvāṅku paṟaiyiṉ viṭarakar tiyampak
       katirkkāl ampiṇai uṇīiya pukalēṟu
       kutirkkāl iruppai veṇpū uṇṇātu         5
       āṇkural viḷikkum cēṇpāl 1viyaṉcuraip
       paṭumaṇi iṉanirai uṇīiya kōvalar
       viṭunilam uṭaitta kaluḻ 2kaṭ kūval
       kaṉṟuṭai maṭappiṭi kaḷiṟoṭu taṭavarum
       puṉṟalai maṉṟat taṅkuṭic cīṟūrt         10
       tuṇaiyoṭu tuccil irukkuṅ kollō
       kaṇaiyōr añcāk kaṭuṅkaṭ kāḷaiyoṭu
       elli muṉṉuṟac celluṅ kollō
       evviṉai ceyuṅkol nōkō yāṉē
       aripeytu potinta tericilampu kaḻīi         15
       3yāyaṟi vuṟutal añci
       vēyuyar piṟaṅkal malaiyin tōḷē.
               ----- kayamaṉār.

   ------------
   (pāṭam) 1. viyaṉ cuval. 2. kaṟkūval.
   3. yāyaṟintuṇartal añci.
   -----------
   (co - ḷ) 15 - 7. ari peytu potinta tericilampu kaḻīi varaliṉaiyiṭṭu
   mūṭiya ārāyntu koṇṭa cilampiṉai nīkki, yāy aṟivuṟutal añci - taṉ tāy
   aṟitalai añci, vēy uyar piṟaṅkal malai iṟantōḷ - mūṅkil uyarnta
   pakkamalaiyaiyuṭaiya malaiyait taṉ talaivaṉuṭaṉ kaṭantu ceṉṟa eṉ makaḷ,
   1 - 6. pacitta yāṉaip paḻaṅkaṇ aṉṉa - paciyuṟṟa yāṉaiyiṉ polivaṟṟa
   kaṇpōṉṟa, vaṟu cuṉai mukanta kōṭait teḷviḷi -nīraṟṟa cuṉaiyuṭ
   pukuntuvarum mēlkāṟṟiṉ teḷinta oli, vicittu vāṅku paṟaiyiṉ viṭar akattu
   iyampa - tōliṉai iḻuttuk kaṭṭiya paṟaiyolipōla malaippiḷappil olikka,
   katirkāl ampiṇai uṇīiya - melintu nīṇṭa kāliṉaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya peṇmāṉ
   uṇṇutaṟku, pukal ēṟu - ataṉpāl viruppam mikka kalaimāṉ, kutirkāl
   iruppai veṇ pū uṇṇātu - kutirpōṉṟa aṭimaratti ṉaiyuṭaiya iruppai
   marattiṉ veḷḷiya pūviṉait tāṉ uṇṇātu, āṇ kural viḷikkum - taṉatu
   āṇkural tōṉṟa ataṉai aḻaikkum, cēṇpāl - cēymaikkaṇṇuḷḷa,
   6 - 10. viyaṉ curai paṭumaṇi iṉam nirai uṇīiya - periya maṭiyiṉaiyum
   olikkum maṇiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya kūṭṭamāya āṉiraikaḷ paruka, kōvalar - āyar,
   viṭunilam uṭaitta kaluḻkaṇ kūval - taricu nilattē kaṟkaḷai uṭaittu
   iyaṟṟiya pilkum ūṟṟuk kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiya kiṇaṟṟiṉai, kaṉṟu uṭai maṭappiṭi
   kaḷiṟoṭu taṭavarum - kaṉṟiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷaiyapiṭi taṉ kaḷiṟṟuṭaṉ kūṭit
   taṭavip pārkkum, puṉtalai maṉṟattu am kuṭi cīṟūr - polivaṟṟa
   iṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya maṉṟiṉaiyum aḻakiya kuṭiyiruppiṉaiyum uṭaiya cīṟūriṉ
   kaṇṇē,
   11 - 4. elli tuṇaiyoṭu tuccil irukkuṅkol - iravil taṉ tuṇaivaṉuṭaṉ ōr
   otukkiṭattē taṅkiyiruppāḷō (aṉṟi avviravil), kaṇaiyōr añcāk kaṭuṅkaṇ
   kāḷaiyoṭu - ampiṉai eyyum āṟalaippōrai añcāta taṟukaṇmaiyaiyuṭaiya
   kāḷaiyāya taṉ talaivaṉuṭaṉ, muṉ uṟac cellumkol - avaṉ muṉṉē naṭantu
   celluvāḷō? e viṉai ceyum kol - eṉṉa kāriyattiṉaic ceyvaḷō? yāṉ nōku -
   yāṉ nōvēṉākiṉṟēṉ.
   (muṭipu) yāy aṟivuṟutalañci cilampu kaḻīi malaiyiṟantōḷ, cēṇ pāl cīṟūr
   elli tuṇaiyoṭu tuccil irukkuṅkol, kāḷaiyoṭu muṉṉuṟac celluṅkol, evviṉai
   ceyuṅkol? yāṉ nōku.
   (vi - rai.) paciyāl vāṭiya yāṉaiyiṉ kaṇ kuḻintu pacaiyiṉṟiyiruttaliṉ
   ataṉai vaṟuñcuṉaikku uvamai kūṟiṉār. vicittu vāṅkupāṟai - kaṭippiṉai
   iḻuttu olippikkum iḻuku paṟai eṉṟumām. iruppaiyiṉ pū, piṇaiyum tāṉum
   uṇpataṟkup pōtiyataṉṟākaliṉ, piṇaiyai māttiram uṇpikka vēṇṭiya
   kalaimāṉ, tāṉ ataṉai uṇṇāmalē piṇaiyai viḷikkum eṉṟār. āṇkural viḷikkum
   eṉṟatu, piṇai ētamiṉṟeṉat tuṇintu varutaṟkut taṉatu āṇmai taṉmaiyuṭaiya
   kuralāl viḷikkum eṉṟapaṭi. cēṇpāluḷḷa cīṟūr eṉak kūṭṭuka. viyaṉ cuval
   eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ periya kaḻuttiṉ kaṇ (poruntiya maṇi) eṉṟuraikka,
   viṭunilam - payir ceyyātu viṭappaṭṭa nilam. kaṉṟuṭai maṭappiṭi kaḷiṟoṭu
   taṭavarum eṉṟataṉāl avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟēṉum uṇpataṟku nīrillāmai peṟṟām. tuccil
   - piṟar maṉaiyil uḷḷa otukkiṭam. ittakaiya kāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa cīṟūril
   irāppoḻutu tuṇaivaṉoṭu tuccil iruttal aṉṟi avaṉuṭaṉ naṭantu cellutal
   āya iraṇṭum tuṉpam viḷaippaṉavē eṉpāḷ evviṉai ceyuṅkol nōkō yāṉē eṉṟāḷ.
   --------------
   322. kuṟiñci
   [allakuṟippaṭṭup pōkiṉṟa talaimakaṉ taṉ ṉeñciṟkuc colliyatu.]
   vayaṅkuveyil ñemiyap pāay miṉṉuvacipu
       mayaṅkutuḷi poḻinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkul
       ārāk kāmam aṭūuniṉ ṟalaippa
       iṟuvarai vīḻnariṉ naṭuṅkit teṟuvarap
       pāmpeṟi kōliṟ ṟamiyai vaikit         5
       tēmputi kollō neñcē urumicaik
       kaḷiṟukaṇ kūṭiya vāṇmayaṅku ñāṭpiṉ
       oḷiṟuvēṟ ṟāṉaik kaṭuntērt titiyaṉ
       varupuṉal iḻitaru marampayil iṟumpiṟ
       piḻaiyuṟaḻ maruppiṟ kaṭuṅkaṭ 1paṉṟik         10
       kuṟaiyār koṭuvari kuḻumuñ cāral
       aṟaiyuṟu tīntēṉ kuṟavar aṟuppa
       muyalunar muṟṟā ēṟṟaru neṭuñcimaip
       pukalarum potiyil 2pōlap
       peṟalaruṅ kuraiyaṇam aṇaṅki yōḷē.         15
               ---- paraṇar.

   -----------
   (pāṭam) 1. paṉṟi kuṟaiyāk koṭuvari. 2. pōlum.
   ----------
   (co - ḷ.) 6. neñcē!
   15. nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ - nammai iṅṅaṉam varuttiya nam kātali,
   6 - 15. urum icai kaḷiṟu kaṇkūṭiyavāḷ mayaṅku ñāṭpiṉ - iṭipōṉṟa
   muḻakkiṉaiyuṭaiya āṇyāṉaikaḷ tiraṇṭavāṭkaḷ piṉṉiya pōrkkaṇṇē, oḷiṟu vēl
   tāṉai kaṭumtēr titiyaṉ - viḷaṅkum vēliṉaiyuṭaiya cēṉaiyiṉaiyum kaṭiya
   tēriṉaiyum uṭaiya titiyaṉ eṉpāṉatu, varupuṉal iḻitaru maram payil
   iṟumpil - malaiyiṉiṉṟu varum aruvi viḻum maraṅkaḷ neruṅkiya kāṭṭil,
   piṟai uṟaḻ maruppiṉ kaṭuṅkaṇ paṉṟi - piṟaimatiyiṉai yotta kōṭṭiṉaiyum
   añcāmaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya paṉṟiyiṉatu, kuṟai ār koṭuvari kuḻumum cāral -
   iṟaiccit tuṇṭiṉait tiṉṟa pulikaḷ kūṭi muḻaṅkum pakkamalaiyiṉaiyum, aṟai
   uṟu tīm tēṉ aṟuppa muyalunar kuṟavar - uccippāṟaiyil uḷḷa iṉiya tēṉai
   aṟutteṭukka muyalum kuṟavarkaḷum, muṟṟā - eytiyaṟiyāta, ēṟṟu aruneṭu
   cimai - ēṟutal ariya nīṇṭa uccimalaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya, pukal arum potiyil
   pōl - pukutaṟ kariya potiyil malaiyiṉaippōla, peṟala aruṅkuraiyaḷ -
   peṟutaṟku arumai yāyavaḷ aṉṟē! (ataṉai uṇarātu,)
   1-6. vayaṅku veyil ñemiya pāay - viḷaṅkum veyil maṟaiyavē (mēkam)
   parantu, miṉṉu vacipu - miṉṉal piḷantiṭa, mayaṅku tuḷi poḻinta - mikka
   maḻaiyaic corinta, pāṉāṭ kaṅkul - naṭu iravil, ārāk kāmam aṭūu niṉṟu
   alaippa - amaiyāta kāmam varuttā niṉṟu alaintiṭa, iṟu varai- vīḻnariṉ -
   malai mukaṭṭiṉiṉṟu vīḻntiṟappār pōla, teṟu vara naṭuṅki, - accam uṇṭāka
   naṭuṅki, pāmpu eṟi kōlil - kōlāl aṭikkappaṭṭa pāmpupōl, tamiyai vaiki -
   vēṟu tuṇaiyiṉṟiyiruntu, tēmputi kollō - vāṭuvaiyō.
   (muṭipu) neñcē! nam aṇaṅkiyōḷ, potiyil pōlap peṟalaruṅ kuraiyāḷ;
   (ataṉai uṇarātu) pāṉāṭ kaṅkul ārāk kāmam aṭūu niṉṟalaippa, naṭuṅki,
   kōleṟi pāmpiṉ tēmputi kollō.
   (vi - rai.) mēkam pāay eṉa varuvitturaikka. vacipu - vaciya eṉat
   tirikka. kāmam aṭūu niṉṟu alaippa eṉpataṉai iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalākkoṇṭu,
   alaittalāl vīḻnarpōla eṉavum alaittalāl naṭuṅki eṉavum koḷka. iṟuvarai
   - malaiyiṉ iṟuvāy. pāmpeṟi kōliṉ eṉpataṉaik kōl ēṟi pāmpiṉ eṉa māṟuka.
   aṭiyuṇṭa pāmpu ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭappatupōla, nīyum ceyalaṟṟuk kiṭattiyō
   eṉpāṉ pāmpu eṟi kōliṉ tēmputi kollō eṉṟāṉ. kaḷiṟu kaṇ kūṭiya: kaṇ,
   acai, titiyaṉatu potiyil eṉavum, cāraliṉaiyum cimaiyiṉaiyumuṭaiya
   potiyil eṉavum kūṭṭuka. paṉṟikkuṟai - paṉṟiyiṉ tacai. ārtal - uṇṭal.
   paṉṟi kuṟaiyāk koṭuvari. eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ paṉṟiyaik koṉṟu puli
   (muḻaṅkum) eṉka. tīntēṉ aṟuppa muyalunar kuṟavar muṟṟā eṉak koṇṭu
   kūṭṭuka. kuṟavarum muṟṟā eṉac ciṟappummai tokkatākak koḷka.
   -----------
   323. pālai
   [piriviṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷait tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.]
   immeṉ pēralar ivvūr namvayiṟ
       ceyvōr ēccol vāṭak kātalar
       varuvar eṉpatu 1vāyva tākal
       aiya ceyya mataṉila ciṟiyaniṉ
       aṭinilaṉ uṟutal añcip paiyat         5
       taṭavaral otukkan takaikoḷa iyalik
       kāṇiya vammō kaṟpumēm paṭuvi
       palavuppala taṭaiiya vēypayi laṭukkattu
       yāṉaic celliṉama kaṭuppa vāṉattu
       vayaṅkukatir maḻuṅkap pā ayp pāmpiṉ         10
       paipaṭa iṭikkuṅ kaṭuṅkural ēṟṟoṭu
       āli yaḻituḷi talaiik
       kālvīḻt taṉṟuniṉ katuppuṟaḻ puyalē.
               ----- puṟanāṭṭup peruṅ koṟṟaṉār.

   -----------
   (pāṭam) 1. vāyvatāka, vaiya.
   -----------
   (co - ḷ.) 7. kaṟpu mēmpaṭuvi - kaṟpiṉāl mēmpaṭṭa talaiviyē, 8-13.
   palavu palataṭaiiya - palāmaraṅkaḷ pala tiraṇṭuḷḷa, vēy payil aṭukkattu
   - mūṅkil neruṅkiya pakka malaiyil, yāṉai cel iṉam kaṭuppa - cellukiṉṟa
   yāṉaik kūṭṭattiṉaip pōla, vāṉattu - vāṉiṉ kaṇ, vayaṅku katir maḻuṅkap
   pāay - viḷaṅkum ñāyiṟu maḻuṅkumāṟu paravi, pāmpiṉ paipaṭa iṭikkum kaṭu
   kural ēṟṟoṭu - pāmpiṉatu paṭam aḻiya iṭikkum kaṭiya kuraliṉaiyuṭaiya
   iṭiyēṟṟuṭaṉ, āvi aḻi tuḷi talaii - nīrkkaṭṭiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya mikka
   tuḷikaḷaip peytu, niṉ katuppu uṟaḻ puyal - niṉ kūntalai yotta mēkam,
   kāl vīḻttaṉṟu - kāl iṟakkiyatu;
   1 - 3. im eṉ pēr alar - immeṉṟu eḻum periya alariṉai, iūr - ivvūriṉkaṇ,
   namvayiṉ ceyvōr - nampāṟ ceyvōratu, ē col vāṭa - cerukkuṟṟa col aḻiya,
   kātalar varuvar eṉpatu - nam kātalar vantu viṭuvar eṉpatu, vāyvatu ākal
   - uṇmai yātalai,
   4 - 7. niṉ - niṉatu, aiya ceyya mataṉ ila ciṟiya aṭi - aḻakiya civanta
   valiyaṟṟa ciṟiya aṭikaḷ, nilaṉ uṟutal añci - nilattiṟ poruntutalai
   añci, paiya - melleṉa, taṭavaral otukkam takaikoḷa iyali - uṭal
   vaḷaintu naṭakkum naṭai aḻaku peṟa naṭantu, kāṇiya vammō - kāṇutaṟku
   varuvāyāka.
   (muṭipu) kaṟpu mēmpaṭuvi, puyalkāl vīḻttaṉṟu; pēr alar nam vayiṟ
   ceyvōr, ēccol vāṭak kātalar varuvareṉpatu vāyvatākal, takaikoḷa iyali,
   kāṇiya vammō.
   (vi - rai.) immeṉ pēralar - iṭaiviṭāta periya alar eṉka. ēccol -
   ēkkaḻuttamuṭaiya col, tarukkuṭaiya col. vāṭutal - oḻital. vāyvatākal
   kāṇiya vammō eṉavum taṉai koḷa iyali vammō eṉavum kūṭṭuka. taṭavaral -
   vaḷaivu. otukkam - naṭai. vāṉattu vayaṅku katir eṉavum, vāṉattup pāay
   eṉavum vāṉam eṉpatu īriṭattum ceṉṟu iyaiyum. puyal kāl vīḻttaṉṟu
   ātalāl, iṉi, kārkālat toṭakkattē kātalar varutal uṇmai eṉa vēṉiliṉ
   iṟutikkaṇ tōḻi talaiviyai vaṟpuṟuttiṉāḷ eṉka.
   ------------
   324. mullai
   [viṉaimuṟṟiya talaimakaṉ karuttuṇarntu uḻaiyar colliyatu.]
   viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlun tiruntiḻait
       taṭameṉ paṇaittōḷ maṭamoḻi yarivai
       taḷiriyaṟ kiḷḷai iṉitiṉ eṭatta
       1vaḷarāp piḷḷait tūvi yaṉṉa
       2vārpeyal vaḷartta paimpayirp puṟaviṟ         5
       paṟaikkaṇ aṉṉa niṟaiccuṉai tōṟun
       tuḷipaṭu mokkuḷ tuḷḷuvaṉa cālat
       toḷiporu pokuṭṭut tōṉṟuvaṉa māya
       vaḷiciṉai yutirttaliṉ veṟikoḷpu tāayc
       ciraṟciṟa kēyppa aṟaṟkaṇ varitta         10
       vaṇṭuṇ naṟuvī tumitta nēmi
       taṇṇila maruṅkiṟ pōḻnta vaḻiyuḷ
       niraicel pāmpiṉ viraipunīr muṭukac
       cellum neṭuntakai tērē
       mullai mālai nakarpukal āyntē.         15
               ----- okkūr mācāttiyār.

   ----------
   (pāṭam) 1. vaḷariḷam piḷḷai. 2. uḷar peyal.
   -------------
   (co - ḷ.) 3 - 15. taḷir iyal kiḷḷai - taḷiriṉ iyal vāynta kiḷi, iṉitiṉ
   eṭutta - iṉitu vaḷartta, vaḷarāp piḷḷai tūvi aṉṉa - iḷaiya pārppiṉ
   ciṟakiṉai yotta, vār peyal vaḷartta pai payir puṟavil - peyyum maḻai
   vaḷartta paciya payiriṉaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭil, paṟai kaṇ aṉṉa niṟaiccuṉai
   tōṟum - paṟaiyiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa niṟaitalai yuṭaiya cuṉaikaḷ tōṟum, tuḷipaṭu
   mokkukaḷ tuḷḷuvaṉa cāla - maḻai peytalāl uṇṭākiya kumiḻikaḷ
   kutippaṉavaṟṟaiyoppa, toḷiporu pokuṭṭu tōṉṟuvaṉa māya - cēṟṟiṉ kaṇṇē
   porutalāl eḻum kumiḻikaḷ tōṉṟi maṟaiya, vaḷiciṉai utirttaliṉ veṟi koḷpu
   tāay - ciṉaiyiṉiṉṟum kāṟṟu utirttalāl maṇam koṇṭu parantu, ciral ciṟaku
   ēyppa aṟal kaṇ varitta - ciccilip paṟavaiyiṉ ciṟakiṉai yoppa
   aṟaliṉiṭattuk kōlam ceyta, vaṇṭu uṇ naṟuvī tumitta nēmi - vaṇṭukaḷ tēṉ
   uṇṇum naṟiya pūkkaḷai aṟuttu ēkiya tēruruḷ, taṇ nila maruṅkil pōḻnta
   vaḻiyuḷ - kuḷirnta nilattiṭattē piḷantu ceṉṟa cuvaṭṭil, nirai celpāmpiṉ
   viraipu nīr muṭuka - oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka varicai yuṟṟuc cellum pāmpupōla
   viraintu nīrmuṭukic cella, mullai mālai nakar pukal āyntu - mullai
   malarum mālaik kālattē, nakariṉkaṇ pukutalai āyntuṇarntu, neṭuntakai
   tēr cellum - peruntakaiyāya talaivaṉ tēr cellāniṟkum.
   1-2. tiruntu iḻai taṭamel paṇaitōḷ maṭamoḻi arivai - tiruntiya
   aṇiyiṉaiyum periya meṉmai vāynta mūṅkil pōlum tōḷiṉaiyum maṭappam
   vāynta moḻiyiṉaiyu muṭaiya talaivi, viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum - iṉṟu
   viruntayartalum peṟuvāḷ pōlum.
   (muṭipu) neṭuntakai tēr, puṟavil, toḷi poru poruṭṭut tōṉṟuvaṉa māya,
   nēmi pōḻnta vaḻiyuḷ nirai cel pāmpiṉ viraipu nir muṭuka, mālai, nakar
   pukal āyntu cellum; arivai viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum.
   (vi - rai.) arivai viruntum peṟukunaṉ pōlum eṉka. - viruntum : ummai
   ciṟappu. vaḷar iḷam piḷḷai eṉṉum pāṭattiṟku vaḷartaṟkuriya iḷam piḷḷai
   eṉka. cāla - oppa eṉṉum poruṭṭu - toḷi - cēṟu. pokuṭṭu - kalaṅkal nīril
   eḻum kumiḻi. talaivaṉ tēr ēṟic celvaṉ eṉṟataṉai, talaivaṉatu tēr cellum
   eṉṟār. cellum ākaliṉ viruntum peṟukunaḷ pōlum eṉa iyaitturaikka.
   (mē - ḷ.)1'āṟṟatu paṇpum’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, talaivaṉ mīḷuṅkāl viruntu
   peṟukuvaḷ kolleṉa iḷaiyōr talaivi nilaiyuraittalukku icceyyuḷaik
   kāṭṭiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

   1. tol. kaṟ: 29

   325. pālai
   [1koṇṭu nīṅkak karuti yoḻinta talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ talaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc
   colliyatu.]
   amma vāḻi tōḻi kātalar
       veṇmaṇal nivanta polaṅkaṭai neṭunakar
       naḷiyiruṅ kaṅkuṟ puṇarkuṟi vāytta
       kaḷavuṅ kaimmika 2vaḷarntaṉ ṟaṉṉaiyum
       uṭkoṇ ṭōvāḷ kākkum piṟperitu         5
       ivaṇuṟai vevaṉō aḷiyaḷeṉ ṟaruḷi
       āṭunaṭaip polinta pukaṟciyiṉ 3nāṭukōḷ
       aḷḷaṉaip paṇitta atiyaṉ piṉṟai
       vaḷḷuyir mākkiṇai 4kaṇṇavin tāṅku
       malaikaviṉ aḻinta kaṉaikaṭaṟ ṟaruñcuram         10
       veyya maṉṟaniṉ vaipeyi ṟuṇīiya
       taṇmaḻai yorunāḷ talaiiya oṇṇutal
       olkiyal arivai niṉṉoṭu celkañ
       ciṉṉāḷ āṉṟaṉai yākeṉap paṉṉāḷ
       ulaivil uḷḷamoṭu viṉaivali 5yuṟīi         15
       ellām perumpiṟi tāka varalāṟu
       6nalvēṟ pāṇaṉ naṉṉāṭ ṭuḷḷatai
       vāṭkaṇ vāṉat teṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭai
       āṭkol yāṉai atarpārt talkum
       cōlai attam mālaip pōki         20
       oḻiyac ceṉṟōr maṉṟa
       paḻiyevaṉ āṅkol nōytaru pālē.
               ----- māmūlaṉār.

   ----
   (pāṭam) 1. talaimakaṉ kuṟippaṟinta tōḻi, talaimakaṭkuc colliyatu.
   2. valantaṉṟu. 3. nāṭu, koḷḷaip paṇitta.
   4. kaṇṇavintaṉa. 5. uṟīiya.
   6. palvēṟ pāṇaṉ.
   --------------
   (co-ḷ.)1-6. tōḻi-, vāḻi-, amma-yāṉ kūṟuvataṉaik kēṭpāyāka, kātalar -
   nam kātalar, veṇmaṇal nivanta-veḷḷiya maṇal uyarnta, polaṉ kaṭai neṭu
   nakar - polivu peṟṟa vāyilaiyuṭaiya periya maṉaiyiṉkaṇ, naḷi iru kaṅkul
   - iruḷ ceṟinta nīṇṭa iravil, puṇar kuṟi vāytta - puṇarum kuṟi amainta,
   kaḷavum-kaḷavoḻukkamum, kaimika vaḷarntaṉṟu-varampikantu nīṇṭatu,
   aṉṉaiyum-, uṭkoṇṭu-ik kaḷaviṉai uḷḷattiṟkoṇṭu, ovāḷ
   kākkum-nīṅkātavaḷākik kāval ceytirukkum, piṉ peritu ivaṇ uṟaivu evaṉ -
   piṉṉar mikavum iṅkut taṅkiyiruttal eṅṅaṉam iyalvatākum, aḷiyaḷ -
   iraṅkattakkāḷ, eṉṟu aruḷi - eṉṟu irakkamuṟṟu,
   7-14. āṭu naṭaip polinta pukaṟciyiṉ - veṉṟi yoḻukkattāl mēmpaṭṭa
   viruppattiṉāl, aḷḷaṉai nāṭukōḷ paṇitta - aḷḷaṉ eṉpāṉai nāṭṭiṉaik
   koḷḷumāṟu paṇitta, atiyaṉ piṉṟai - atiyaṉ eṉpāṉ tuñciya piṉpu, vaḷ uyir
   mā kiṇaikaṇ avintāṅku - ciṟanta oliyiṉaiyuṭaiya periya kiṇaiyāṉatu oli
   aṭaṅkiṉāṟpōla, malai kaviṉ aḻinta kaṉai kaṭaṟṟu arucuram - malaiyiṉ
   aḻaku oḻinta ceṟinta kāṭākiya ariya pālaivaḻikaḷ, veyya maṉṟa - oru
   talaiyākak koṭiyaṉa, niṉ vai eyiṟu uṇīiya - niṉ kūriya paṟkaḷ nīr
   parukumāṟu, taṇ maḻai orunāḷ talai iya - kuḷirntamaḻai orunāḷ
   peyvatāka, oḷ nutal olku iyal arivai - oḷiporuntiya neṟṟiyiṉaiyum
   taḷarum iyaliṉaiyumuṭaiya arivaiyē, niṉṉoṭu celkam - piṉ niṉṉuṭaṉ
   celvēm, cilanāḷ āṉṟaṉai āka eṉa - cila nāḷ amaintiruppāyāka eṉṟu,
   14-6. pal nāḷ ulaivu il uḷḷamoṭu - palakālam taḷartalillāta ūkkattuṭaṉ,
   viṉai vali yuṟīi - koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalākiya viṉaiyai vaṟpuṟutturaittu,
   ellām perum piṟitāka - avaiyellām aḻintupōka,
   16-21. vaṭāatu - vaṭakkiṉkaṇ, nal vēlpāṇaṉ nalnāṭṭuḷḷatai - nalla
   vēlaiyuṭaiya pāṇaṉatu ciṟanta nāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷatāya, vāḷ kaṇ vāṉattu eṉṟūḻ
   nīḷ iṭai - oḷiporuntiya vāṉiṉ kaṇṇākiya ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmai mikka nīṇṭa
   curattilē, āḷ kolyāṉai - neṟiccelvōrkaḷaik koṉṟiṭum yāṉai, atar pārttu
   alkum - vaḻiyiṉaip pārttut taṅkiyirukkum, cōlai attam mālaipōki -
   cōlaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāṭṭu neṟiyil mālaikkālattē pōy, oḻiya ceṉṟōr - nām
   ivaṇ oḻintirukkacceṉṟār ātaliṉ,
   21-2. nōy taru pāl - nōyiṉaic ceyyum pakutiyāl, maṉṟa - orutalaiyāka,
   paḻi evaṉ ām kol - yātu pā uṇṭākumō.
   (muṭipu) tōḻi! vāḻi! kātalar, kaḷavuṅ kaimika vaḷarntaṉṟu, aṉṉaiyum
   uṭkoṇṭu ōvāḷ kākkum; piṉperitu ivaṇ uṟaivu evaṉō; aḷiyaḷ; eṉṟaruḷi,
   aruñcuram veyya niṉeyiṟu uṇīiya maḻai orunāḷ talaiiya; arivai! niṉṉoṭu
   celkam; ciṉṉāḷ āṉṟaṉaiyākeṉa viṉaivali yuṟīi, ellām perumpiṟitāka,
   eṉṟūḻ nīḷiṭai attam mālaip pōki oḻiyac ceṉṟōr; nōytarupāl maṉṟa paḻi
   evaṉ āṅkol.
   (vi - rai.) kaṅkuṟ puṇar kuṟi vāytta eṉṟatu, iravuk kuṟikkaṇ puṇartal
   amainta eṉṟapaṭi. 'āṭunaṭai......atiyaṉ’ eṉpatiliruntu, aḷḷaṉ eṉpāṉ
   taṉakku veṟṟiyai aḷittāṉ eṉṟa makiḻcciyāl, atiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ avaṉukkup
   paricilāka nāṭu nalkiṉaṉ pōlum eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. atiyaṉ tuñciya
   piṉpu eṉpatu kiṇai kaṇṇavin tāṅku eṉpataṉāṟ peṟṟām. kaṇ - kiṇaiyiṉ
   aṭikkappaṭum iṭam. kaṇ avital - aṭipaṭā toḻital, kiṇai kaṇṇavintāṅku
   malai kaviṉaḻinta eṉka. eyiṟu: vāykku āku peyar. perum piṟitāka -
   aḻintoḻiya. vāṭkaṇ vāṉattu, oḷi poruntiya vāṉiṉ kaṇ eṉka. vāṉkaṇ -
   ñāyiṟu; 1'vāṉkaṇ viḻiyāvaikaṟai yāmattu’ eṉpatu kāṇka. namakku nōyaic
   ceyyum pakutiyāl avarukku yātu paḻiyuṇṭākumō eṉṟu talaivi tōḻikkuk
   kūṟiṉāḷ eṉka. namakku varum paḻi eṉṉai eṉṟāḷum ām.

   1. cilap. 10: 1.

   326. marutam
   [tōḻi talaimakaṉai vāyil maṟuttatu.]
   2ūral avvāy urutta tittip
       pēramar maḻaikkaṭ peruntōṭ ciṟunutal
       nallaḷ amma kuṟumakaḷ celvar
       kaṭuntēr kuḻitta ñeḷḷal āṅkaṇ
       neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅkum aṭṭavāyil         5
       iruṅkatirk kaḻaṉip peruṅkaviṉ aṉṉa
       nalampā rāṭṭi naṭaiyeḻiṟ polintu
       viḻaviṟ celīiyar vēṇṭum veṉvēl
       iḻaiyaṇi yāṉaic cōḻaṉ maṟavaṉ
       kaḻaiyaḷan taṟiyāk kāvirip paṭappaip         10
       3puṉaṉmali putaviṟ pōor kiḻavōṉ
       paḻaiyaṉ ōkkiya vēlpōṟ
       piḻaiyala kaṇṇavaḷ nōkkiyōr tiṟattē.
               -----paraṇar.

   -------
   (pāṭam) 2. ūṟa lavvāy. 3. puṉaṉmali puṟaviṟ.
   -----------
   (co - ḷ.) 1-3. ūral a vāy urutta titti - ūralākiya aḻakuvāynta
   uruppeṟṟa tēmalaiyum, pēr amar maḻai kaṇ - periya amar ceyyum kuḷirnta
   kaṇkaḷaiyum, perutōḷ ciṟu nutal - periya tōḷiṉaiyum ciṟiya neṟṟiṉaiyum
   uṭaiya, kuṟumakaḷ - niṉ iḷaiya parattai, nallaḷa amma - aḻakiṟ
   ciṟantāḷāvaḷ; (ākaliṉ, talaiva!)
   8 - 13. veṉ vēl iḻai aṇi yāṉaicōḻaṉ maṟavaṉ - veṟṟi poruntiya vēlaiyum
   aṇikalaṉ aṇinta yāṉaiyaiyumuṭaiya cōḻaṉiṉ paṭaittalaivaṉum, kaḻai
   aḷanta aṟiyā kāviri paṭappai - ōṭak kōlāl āḻam aḷantaṟiyappaṭāta
   kāviriyiṉ karaiyiṉaic cārnta tōṭṭaṅkaḷaiyum, puṉalmali putaviṉ - nīr
   niṟaintu oṭum matakukaḷaiyum uṭaiya, pōor kiḻavōṉ - pōr eṉṉum ūrukkut
   talaivaṉumākiya, paḻaiyaṉ - eṉpāṉ, ōkkiyavēl pōla - pakaivar mītu
   celuttiya vēl pōla, avaḷ nōkkiyōr tiṟattu - avaḷāṟ pārkkap peṟṟār
   pakkal, kaṇ piḻaiyala - avaḷ kaṇkaḷ taittal tappā;
   3-8. celvar kaṭuntēr kuḻitta ñeḷḷal āṅkaṇ - celvarkaḷatu viraintu
   cellum tēr kuḻitta terukkaḷiṭattē, neṭukoṭi nuṭaṅkum aṭṭavāyil - nīṇṭa
   koṭikaḷ acaiyum aṭṭavāyil eṉṉum ūriṭattēyuḷḷa, iru katir kaḻaṉi
   perukaviṉ aṉṉa - nīṇṭa katirkaḷuṭaiya vayalkaḷiṉ periya aḻakiṉai yotta,
   nalam pārāṭṭi - avaḷatu aḻakiṉaip pārāṭṭi, naṭaiyāl viḷakkamuṟṟu,
   viḻavil celīiyar vēṇṭum - avaḷuṭaṉ puṉal viḻāviṉkaṇ cellutal niṉakkut
   takuvākum.
   (muṭipu) talaiva! kuṟumakaḷ nallaḷ amma; pōor kiḻavōṉ paḻaiyaṉ ōkkiya
   vēlpōla, avaḷ nōkkiyōr tiṟattuk kaṇ piḻaiyala; avaḷ nalam pārāṭṭi,
   naṭai eḻiṟ polintu, viḻaviṟ celīiyar vēṇṭum.
   (vi - rai.) ūral - tēmal vakai. ūralākiya titti eṉka. amma: acai.
   kuṟumakaḷ nallaḷ eṉṟamaiyāl, yām mutumaiyum aḻakiṉmaiyum iṭaiyēm eṉap
   pulantu kūṟiyavāṟu. nuṭaṅkum maṭṭavāyil eṉap pirittaṟkum iṭam uṇṭu.
   naṭai eḻiṟpolintu eṉṟatu, perumitattuṭaṉ iruvarum oruṅku naṭakkum
   naṭaiyil viḷakka muṟṟu eṉṟapaṭi. celīiyar: toḻiṟpeyar. paḻaiyaṉ
   pakaivariṭattu ōkkiya vēl piḻaiyāmai 1'pōr kiḻavōṉ, paḻaiyaṉ vēlvāyt
   taṉṉaniṉ, piḻaiyā naṉmoḻi’ eṉap piṟāṇṭum varutal kāṇka. piḻaiyala kaṇ
   eṉṟatu, nī avaḷai viṭṭu nīṅkutal aritu eṉṟapaṭi. -------
   1. naṟ. 10.

   327. pālai
   [poruḷ kaṭaik kūṭṭiya 2neñciṉaik kaḻaṟiyatu.]
   iṉpamum iṭumpaiyum puṇarvum pirivum
       naṉpakal amaiyamum iravum pōla
       vēṟuvē ṟiyala ākimā ṟetirntu
       uḷaveṉa uṇarntaṉai yāyiṉ orūum
       iṉṉā veñcuram naṉṉacai turappat         5
       tuṉṉalun takumō tuṇivil neñcē
       nīcela valittaṉai yāyiṉ yāvatum
       3niṉaitaluñ ceytiyō emmē kaṉaikatir
       āvi avvari nīreṉa 4nacaii
       mātavap parikkum maraltiraṅku naṉantalaik         10
       kaḷarkāl yātta kaṇṇakaṉ parappiṟ
       cevvaraik koḻinīr kaṭuppa araviṉ
       avvari yurivai aṇavaru maruṅkiṟ
       puṟṟarai yātta pularciṉai maratta
       mainniṟa uruviṉ maṇikkaṭ kākkai         15
       painniṇaṅ kavarum paṭupiṇak kavalaic
       ceṉṟōr celpuṟat tiraṅkār koṉṟōr
       kōlkaḻipu iraṅkum atara
       vēlpayil aḻuvam iṟanta piṉṉē
               ------- maruṅkūrppākaic cāttaṉ pūtaṉār.

   -------
   (pāṭam) 2. neñciṟkuk talaimakaṉ colliyatu.
   3. niṉaitalun niṉaitiyō. 4. nacaiiya.
   ----------------
   (co - ḷ) 6. tuṇivu ilneñcē - uṟuti yaṟṟa neñcamē!
   1 - 6. iṉpamum iṭumpaiyum - iṉpamum tuṉpamum, puṇarvum pirivum -
   puṇartalum piritalum, naṉ pakal amaiyamum iravum pōla - nalla
   pakaṟpoḻutum irāppoḻutum pōla, vēṟu vēṟu iyal āki - vēṟu vēṟu
   taṉmaiyiṉavāki, māṟu etirntu uḷa eṉa - māṟāka etirppaṭṭu niṟpaṉa eṉa,
   uṇarntaṉai āyiṉ - nī uṇarntuḷāyāyiṉ, orūum iṉṉā vem curam - ivaḷai
   nīṅkic cellum iṉṉāmaiyaiyuṭaiya koṭiya curaneṟiyil, nalnacai turappa -
   nallaporuḷ vēṭkai celutta, tuṉṉalum takumō - nī ceṉṟu cērtalum
   takuvatāmā?
   7. nī cela valattaṉai yāyiṉ - nī cellutaṟkē tuṇintuviṭṭāyāyiṉ,
   8 - 19. kaṉai katir āvi av vari - ñāyiṟṟiṉatu mikka katirāl tōṉṟum
   āviyāya avaṟṟiṉ alaikaḷai, nīr eṉa nacaii - nīr eṉṟu eṇṇi virumpi, mā
   tava parikkum - vilaṅkukaḷ mika ōṭi alaiyum, maral tiraṅku naṉantalai -
   maraṟceṭi vāṭum akaṉṟa iṭattiṉaiyum, kaḷar kāl yātta kaṇ akaṉ parappiṉ
   - kaḷar paranta iṭamakaṉṟa parappiṉaiyum, cevvaraik koḻi nīr kaṭuppa -
   ceṅkuttāya malaiyiṉiṉṟum koḻittu varum aruvinīr pōla, araviṉ a vari
   urivai aṇavarum maruṅkiṉ - pāmpiṉatu aḻakiya varikaḷaiyuṭaiya urikkap
   peṟṟa tōlkaḷ poruntum pāṟai iṭaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaiya, puṟṟu arai yātta -
   puṟṟukkaḷ aṭimarattil kaṭṭappeṟṟuḷa, pular ciṉai maratta - kāynta
   kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya marattiṭattavāya, mai niṟa uruviṉ maṇik kaṇ kākkai -
   peyyum mēkattiṉ niṟam pōlum uruviṉaiyum nīlamaṇi pōlum
   kaṇṇiṉaiyumuṭaiya kākkaikaḷ, painiṇam kavarum paṭu piṇa kavalai -
   paciya koḻuppiṉaik kavarntuṇṇum iṭamāya mikka piṇaṅkaḷ kiṭakkum
   kavartta neṟikaḷākiya, ceṉṟōr cel puṟattu iraṅkār koṉṟōr - vaḻiccellum
   vaṇikarkaḷ pōkum iṭaṅkaḷiṉ piṉṉē yiruntu irakkamuṟātu avarkaḷaik koṉṟa
   āṟalaippōr, kōl kaḻipu iraṅkum atara - taṅkaḷ ampukaḷ kaḻintataṉ
   poruṭṭē iraṅkum neṟikaḷaiyuṭaiya, vēy payil aḻuvam iṟantapiṉ - mūṅkil
   neruṅkiya kāṭṭiṉaik kaṭanta piṉ,
   7 - 8. emmē yāvatum niṉaitalum ceytiyō - eṅkaḷaic ciṟitēṉum niṉaittalum
   ceyvāyō.
   (muṭipu) tuṇivil neñcē! iṉpamum tuṉpamum puṇarvum pirivum vēṟu
   vēṟiyalavāki māṟetirntu uḷa eṉa uṇarntaṉaiyāyiṉ veñcuram tuṉṉalum
   takumō; nī celavalittaṉaiyāyiṉ vēy payil aḻuvam iṟanta piṉṉē emmai
   yāvatum niṉaitalum ceytiyō.
   naṉantalaiyiṉaiyum parappiṉaiyum maruṅkiṉaiyum uṭaiya aḻuvam eṉavum
   kavalaiyākiya atarkaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻuvam eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka.
   (vi - rai.) iṉpamum iṭumpaiyum māṟu etirntuḷa, puṇarvum pirivum māṟu
   etirntuḷa eṉka. itaṉaip puṇarvum ataṉālāya iṉpamum, pirivum, ataṉālāya
   iṭumpaiyum eṉa uraittalumām. iṉpamum, iṭumpaiyum pakalum iravum pōla
   eṉa niraṉiṟaiyākak koḷka. talaiviyaip puṇarntum iṉpam tuykkum nī,
   piriyiṉ tuṉpamēyuḷa eṉaṟataṉai aṟintāyāyiṉ poruḷnacaiyāl talaiviyaip
   pirintu veñcuram tuṉṉutal takātu eṉṟāṉ. tuṇivu - teḷivumām. nī cela
   valittaṉaiyāyiṉ - tuṉpamātalai aṟintuvaittum nī cellutalait
   tuṇintāyāyiṉ eṉka. aḻuvam iṟanta piṉ emmai niṉaitalum ceytiyō eṉṟatu,
   yām niṉṉuṭaṉ varuvēm allēm eṉakkūṟic celavaḻuṅkuvitta paṭiyām.
   kālyāttal - parattal: oru col. pāmpiṉ uri aruvi nīr pōlvatātal,
   1''pāmpiṉ, tūṅku tōl kaṭukkun tūveḷ ḷaruvi" eṉpataṉālum peṟappaṭum.

   328. kuṟiñci
   [iravukkuṟic ciṟaippuṟamākat tōḻi colleṭuppat 1talaimakaḷ colliyatu]
   vaḻaiyāmal aṭukkattu valaṉērpu vayiriyar
       muḻavatirn taṉṉa muḻakkattu ēṟōṭu
       uravuppeyal poḻinta naḷḷeṉ yāmattu
       araviṉ paintalai iṭaṟip pāṉāḷ
       iraviṉ vantem 2iṭaimulai poruntit         5
       tuṉikaṇ akala vaḷaiik kaṅkuliṉ
       iṉitiṉ iyainta naṇpavar 3muṉital
       teṟṟā kutaṉaṟ kaṟintaṉa māyiṉ
       ilaṅkuvaḷai nekiḻap parantupaṭar alaippayām
       muyaṅkutoṟum muyaṅkutoṟum uyaṅkamukantukoṇṭu         10
       aṭakkuvam maṉṉō tōḻi maṭappiṭi
       maḻaitavaḻ cilampiṟ kaṭuñcūl īṉṟu
       kaḻaitiṉ yākkai viḻaikaḷiṟu taivara
       vāḻaiyañ cilampiṟ ṟuñcuñ
       cāral nāṭaṉ cāyaṉ mārpē.         15
               ------ maturai paṇṭavāṇikaṉ 4iḷantēvaṉār.

   -----------
   (pāṭam) 1. tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.
   2. iṭaimulai muyaṅki. 3. muṉitō ṟiṟṟā.
   4. īḻaṉṟēvaṉār.
   -------------
   (co - ḷ.) 11. tōḻi
   1-9. vaḻai amal aṭukkattu - curapuṉṉai maraṅkaḷ niṟainta malaiccāralil,
   valaṉ ērpu-(mēkamāṉatu) valamāka eḻuntu, vayiriyar muḻavu atirntaṉṉa
   muḻakkattu ēṟōṭu - kūttaratu muḻavam atirntāṟ pōṉṟa
   muḻakkattiṉaiyuṭaiya iṭayēṟukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi, uravu peyal poḻinta naḷ eṉ
   yāmattu - mikka peyalaic corinta naḷḷeṉṉum oliyiṉaiyuṭaiya naṭu iravil,
   araviṉ paitalai iṭaṟi - pāmpiṉatu paciya talaiyai iṭaṟikkoṇṭu, pāl nāḷ
   iraviṉ vantu - antappāti iravil vantu, emiṭai mulai porunti - em
   mulaiyiṭaiyē porunti, tuṉi kaṇ akal - tamatu varuttam tammiṭattu niṉṟum
   nīṅka, vaḷaii - ākattait taḻuvi, kaṅkuliṉ - iravelām, iṉitiṉa iyainta
   naṇpu - iṅṅaṉam iṉimaiyākap poruntiya naṇpiṉai, ilaṅku vaḷai nekiḻa -
   viḷaṅkum vaḷai nekiḻavum, paṭarparantu alaippa - namatu tuṉpam paravi
   varuttavum, avar muṉital teṟṟu ākutal naṟku aṟintaṉam āyiṉ - avar piṉ
   veṟuttal teḷivātalai nām naṉku uṇarvōmāyiṉ.
   11 - 5. maḻai tavaḻ cilampil - mēkam tavaḻum pakkamalaiyil, maṭapiṭi -
   iḷaiya peṇ yāṉai, kaṭuñ cūl īṉṟu - taṉatu mutaṟcūliṉai īṉṟu, kaḻai tiṉ
   yākkai - mūṅkilait tiṉṉum taṉ yākkaiyiṉai, viḻai kaḷiṟu taivara -
   taṉpāl viruppa mikka taṉatu āṇ yāṉai taṭavikkoṇṭirukka, vāḻai am
   cilampil tuñcum - vāḻaikaḷaiyuṭaiya aḻakiya ap pakka malaiyil tuyilum,
   cāral nāṭaṉ - cāralaiyuṭaiya malaināṭaṉāya at talaivaṉatu, cāyal mārpu
   - meṉmaiyai yuṭaiya mārpiṉai,
   9 - 11. yām - nām, muyaṅkutoṟum muyaṅkutoṟum - avar muyaṅkutōṟum
   muyaṅkuṟutōṟum, uyaṅka mukantukoṇṭu aṭakkuvam - varutta mukantukoṇṭu
   nam mārpiṉkaṇṇē aṭakkiyiruppēm. maṉ - atu kaḻintatē,
   (muṭipu) tōḻi! pāṉāḷ iraviṉ vantu kaṅkuliṉ iyainta naṇpuvaḷai nekiḻap
   paṭar alaippa avar muṉital teṟṟākutal naṟku aṟintaṉamāyiṉ, cāral nāṭaṉ
   mārpu, muyaṅkuṟutoṟum uyaṅka mukantukoṇṭu aṭakkuvam maṉ.
   (vi - rai.) mēkam eṉa varuvittu, mēkam ērpu poḻinta yāmam eṉa muṭikka.
   araviṉ paintalai iṭaṟi eṉpatu iruḷiṉ ceṟivai uṇarttiyapaṭi eṉavē iravuk
   kuṟiyiṉ ētam añcātu vantu eṉpatāyiṟṟu. paintalai, paittalai eṉpataṉ
   vikāram eṉakkoṇṭu paṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya talai eṉṟalumām. akala aḷaii eṉap
   pirintiruttal. uyaṅka mukantukoṇṭu - varuntumpaṭi iṟukap paṟṟik koṇṭu
   eṉṟavāṟu. muyaṅkutoṟum muyaṅka eṉappirittu, mēlum iṭaiviṭātu muyaṅka
   eṉṟuraittalumām. tōḻi colleṭuppat talaimakaḷ colliyatu eṉṉum
   tuṟaikkēṟpa, naṟku aṟintaṉamāyiṉ eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tōḻi kūṟi muṭikkumuṉ,
   nāṭaṉ mārpiṉai avaṉ muyaṅkutōṟum mukantukoṇṭu aṭakkuvam eṉat talaivi
   kūṟiṉāḷ eṉṟu koḷka.
   (u - ṟai.) maṭappiṭi maḻai tavaḻ cilampil cūl īṉṟu yākkai kaḷiṟu
   taivara cilampil tuñcum eṉṟatu, talaivi, talaivaṉuṭaiya celvamikka
   maṉaiyil makappeṟṟu, talaivaṉ aṉpu pārāṭṭa illaṟam naṭattutalai
   virumpuvaḷ eṉṟapaṭi.
   (mē - ḷ.)1'orutalai yurimai vēṇṭiyum’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattu 'vaḻaiyamal
   aṭukkattu’ eṉpataṉuḷ, mukantukoṇṭaṭakkuvam eṉa iṭaiviṭātu iṉpam nukara
   virumpiyavāṟum, uḷḷuṟaiyāṉ illaṟam nikaḻnta virumpiyavāṟum kāṇka eṉṟār,
   nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

   329. pālai
   [piriviṭai vēṟupaṭṭa talaimakaḷ tōḻikkuc colliyatu. pirivuṇarttiya
   tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatūumām.]
   pūṅkaṇum nūtalum pacappa nōykūrntu
       īṅkiyāṉ varuntavum nīṅkutal tuṇintu
       vāḻtal valluna rāyiṟ 1kātalar
       kuvinta kurampai aṅkuṭic cīṟūrp
       paṭumaṇi yiyampap pakaliyaintu umaṇar         5
       koṭunukam piṇitta ceṅkayiṟ ṟoḻukaip
       pakaṭayāk koḷḷum vemmuṉait tukaṭokuttu
       eṟivaḷi cuḻaṟṟum attañ ciṟitacaintu
       ēkuvar kollō tāmē pāykoḷpu
       2uṟuverin oṭikkuñ ciṟuvarik kuruḷai         10
       neṭunal yāṉai nīrnacaik kiṭṭa
       kaikaṟit turaṟum maitūṅku iṟumpil
       pulipukku īṉum vaṟuñcuṉaip
       paṉipaṭu cimaiyap paṉmalai yiṟantē.
               ------uṟaiyūr 3mutukūttaṉār.

   --------
   (pāṭam) 1. ṟōḻi. 2. puṟaveri.
   3. kūṟṟaṉār.
   ----------
   (co - ḷ.) tōḻi! -,
   1 - 3. kātalar - nam kātalar, pū kaṇum nutalum pacappa - eṉatu aḻakiya
   kaṇṇum neṟṟiyum pacalaiyuṟa,nōy kūrntu īṅku yāṉ varuntavum - tuṉpam
   mikku iṅkē yāṉ varuntāniṟkavum, nīṅkutal tuṇintu - nammaip piritalait
   tuṇintu, vāḻtal vallunar āyiṉ - taṉittu vāḻa vaṉmai yuṭaiyarāyiṉ,
   4 - 8. kuvinta kurampai amkuṭic cīṟūr - talai kūmpiya kuṭicaikaḷākiya
   aḻakiya kuṭiyiruppukkaḷai yuṭaiya ciṟṟūriṉkaṇ, paṭu maṇi iyampa -
   poruntiya maṇi olikka, pakal iyaintu - pakuttal porunti, umaṇar
   koṭunukam piṇitta ceṅ kayiṟṟu oḻukaip pakaṭu - uppu vāṇikar vaḷainta
   nukattilē cevviya kayiṟṟiṉāl piṇikkap peṟṟa vaṇṭiyiḻukkum pakaṭukaḷ,
   ayāk koḷḷum - varunti yiḻukkum, vemmuṉai - koṭiya iṭaṅkaḷil eḻum, tukaḷ
   tokuttu - puḻutiyait tiraṭṭi, eṟivaḷi cuḻaṟṟum attam - vīcum kāṟṟuc
   cuḻaṟṟum cura neṟiyil, ciṟitu acaintu - ciṟitu taṅki yiruntu (piṉpu),
   9 - 14. pāy koḷpu uṟu verin oṭikkum ciṟu varikkuruḷai - pāytal koṇṭu
   nērmai poruntiya mutukiṉai neḷikkum kuṟukiya varikaḷaiyuṭaiya
   pulikkuṭṭi, neṭu nal yāṉai nīr nacaikku iṭṭa kaikaṟittu uraṟum -
   nīṇṭuyarnta nalla yāṉai nīr vēṭkaiyāl cuṉaiyiṉ pāl iṭṭa kaiyiṉaik
   kaṭittu muḻaṅkum iṭamāya, mai tūṅku iṟumpil - mēkam tuñcum kāṭṭil,
   pulipukku īṉum vaṟu cuṉai - puli (nīriṉ maiyiṉ) pukuntu kuṭṭiyai īṉum
   vaṟiya cuṉaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya, paṉi paṭucimaiyaip pal maṟai iṟantu. paṉi
   uṟainta ucciyiṉaiyuṭaiya pala malaikaḷaiyum kaṭantu, tām ēkuvar kollō -
   avar pōvarō.
   (muṭipu) tōḻi! kātalar īṅku yāṉ varuntavum nīṅkutal tuṇintu vāḻtal
   vallunarāyiṉ vemmuṉait tukaḷ tokuttu eṟivaḷi cuḻaṟṟum attam ciṟitu
   acaintu paṉmalai yiṟantu ēkuvar kollō.
   (vi - rai.) pūṅkaṇ - pūppōlum kaṇṇum ām. pakal iyaintu piṇitta pakaṭu
   eṉavum, maṇi iyampap pakaṭu ayāk koḷkaḷum eṉavum kūṭṭuka. pakal
   iyaitalāvatu nukattiṉ iru puṟattum tammiṉ ottaṉavāy pakaṭukaḷait tērntu
   koḷḷal. pakaṟpoḻuteṉak koṇṭu pakalilē porunti ayākkoḷḷum eṉṟalumām. eṟi
   vaḷi-cūṟāvaḷi. ciṟitu acaintu - akkāṟṟu niṟkumaḷavum taṅkiyiruntu. pāy
   koḷpu - uṭalai neṭitākkal. nacaikku - nacaiyāl vēṟṟumai mayakkam.

   330. neytal
   [talaimakaṟkuk kuṟainērnta tōḻi talaimakaṭkuk kuṟainayappak kūṟiyatu.]
   kaḻippūk kuṟṟuṅ kāṉal alkiyum
       vaṇṭaṟ pāvai varimaṇal ayarntum
       iṉpuṟap puṇarntum iḷivarap paṇintum
       taṉṟuyar 1veḷippaṭat tavaṟil nantuyar
       aṟiyā maiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu         5
       cellum aṉṉō mellam pulampaṉ
       celvōṉ2 peyarpuṟat tiraṅki muṉṉiṉṟu
       takaiiya ceṉṟaveṉ niṟaiyil neñcam
       eytiṉṟu kollō tāṉē eytiyum
       kāmañ ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō         10
       utuva kāṇavar ūrnta tērē
       kuppai veṇmaṇaṟ kuvavumicai yāṉum
       ekkart tāḻai maṭalvayi ṉāṉum
       āykoṭip pācaṭumpu ariya ūrpiḻipu
       ciṟukuṭip paratavar peruṅkaṭaṉ maṭutta         15
       kaṭuñcelaṟ koṭuntimil pōla
       nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamo ṭikantumā yummē.
               ----- ulōccaṉār.

   -------
   (pāṭam) 1. veḷippaṭal uṟīi. tavaṟi nantuyar.
   2. peyarpuṟat tiyaṅki.
   ---------------
   (co - ḷ.) tōḻi! 6. melam pulāṉ meṉṉilamākiya neytalaiyuṭaiya nam
   talaivaṉ.
   1 - 6. kaḻi pū kuṟṟum - kaḻiyiluḷḷa pūkkaḷaip paṟittum, kāṉal alkiyum -
   kaṭaṟkaraic cōlaiyil taṅkiyiruntum, vaṇṭal pāvai varimaṇal ayarntum -
   varippaṭṭa maṇalil vaṇṭaṟ pālai ceytu viḷaiyāṭiyum, iṉpuuṟa puṇarntum -
   iṉpam uṟap palakāl vantu kūṭiyum, iḷivara paṇintum - ciṟamai tōṉṟap
   paṇitalaic ceytum, tavaṟuil nam tuyar aṟiyāmaiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu -
   kuṟṟamillāta namatu tuyarai aṟiyāmal cōrnta neñcuṭaṉ, taṉ tuyar
   veḷippaṭac cellum - taṉatu tuyaram veḷippaṭṭut tōṉṟac cellā niṟpaṉ,
   aṉṉō - antō!,
   7 -10. celvōṉ peyar puṟattu - aṅṅaṉam celkiṉṟavaṉ pōkiya ticaiyil,
   iraṅki - irakkamuṟṟu, muṉniṉṟu takaiiya - muṉṉē niṉṟu cellaṟka eṉatu
   uṟutiyaṟṟa neñcamāṉatu, eytiṉṟu kollō - at talaivaṉaic ceṉṟa
   cārntatiṉṟō, eytiyum - cārntum, kāmam ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō - nam
   kāmattiṉaik kūṟa nāṇiyatō;
   11 - 7. avar ūrnta tēr utuva kāṇ - avar ēṟicceṉṟa tēr uṅkē tōṉṟalaik
   kāṇpāyāka, kuppai veṇ maṇal kuvavu micaiyāṉum - veḷḷiya maṇala tiraṇṭa
   kuviyalkaḷiṉ mēlum, ekkar tāḻai maṭalvayiṉālum - maṇal mēṭṭiṭattuḷḷa
   tāḻai maṭalkaḷiṉiṭattum, āy koṭi pacu aṭumpu ariya ūrpu iḻivu - aḻakiya
   aṭumpiṉ paciya koṭikaḷ aripaṭa ēṟiyum iṟaṅkiyum, ciṟukuṭip paratavar
   peruṅkaṭal maṭutta - ciṟiya kuṭi vāḻkkaiyaiyuṭaiya neytal mākkaḷ periya
   kaṭaliṟ celuttiya, kaṭu celal koṭu timil pōl - viraintu
   cellutalaiyiṭaiya vaḷainta mīṉ paṭakupōla, nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamoṭu -
   uyarntu tōṉṟum tōṟṟattuṭaṉ, ikantu māyum - ceṉṟu appāl maṟaiyā
   niṟkiṉṟatu.
   (muṭipu) tōḻi! pulampaṉ alkiyum ayarntum puṇarntum paṇintum tavaṟuil
   namtuyar aṟiyāmaiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu cellum; celvōṉ puṟattu iraṅkit
   takaiiya ceṉṟa eṉ neñcam eytiṉṟu kollō; eytiyum kāmam ceppa nāṇiṉṟu
   kollō: avar ūrnta tēr paratavar kaṭal maṭutta timilpōla nivantupaṭu
   tōṟṟamoṭu ikantu māyum ; utuva kāṇ.
   (vi - rai.) taṉ tuyar veḷippaṭac cellum eṉavum, ayarnta neñcamoṭu
   cellum eṉavum taṉittaṉi kūṭṭuka. maṉ ō eṉap pirittu acainilai
   eṉṟalumām. pulampaṉ - neytaṟ ṟalaivaṉ. neytal meṉṉilamākaliṉ neytal
   nilat talaivaṉai mellam pulampaṉ eṉpatu vaḻakkāṟu. eytiṉṟu: etirmaṟaic
   col, 1'pukkil amaintiṉṟu kollō’ eṉpuḻippōla. talaivaṉ ēṟiya tēr
   aṇmaiyil nivanta tōṟṟamuṭaiyatāyiruntu cēymaiyiṟ celluntōṟum
   maṟaitalukku avvāṟu ceṉṟu maṟaiyum timil uvamaiyāyiṟṟu. ippāṭṭiṉait
   talaivi kūṟṟākakkoṇṭu, talaivi taṉvayiṉ urimaiyum avaṉvayiṟ
   parattaimaiyum veḷippaṭak kūṟiyateṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. kaḷavuk
   kālattil, talaivaṉpāl parattaimaiyiṉṟākavum kātal mikutiyāl
   uḷatākakkoṇṭu talaivi ūṭutal uṇṭu eṉpatum atu pulavip pōliyā meṉpatum
   avar karuttākum.
   (mē - ḷ.) 2'maṟaintavaṟ kāṇṭal’ eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, icceyyuḷaik kāṭṭi,
   itaṉuḷ, ''aṟiyāmaiyiṉ ayarnta neñcamoṭu" eṉpatu taṉvayiṉ urimai, ikantu
   māyum eṉpatu avaṉ vayiṟparattaimai, eṉṟu kūṟiṉar, nacciṉārkiṉiyar.

   1. kuṟaḷ, 340 2. tol kaḷa, 20

   331. pālai
   [talaimakaṉ piriviṉkaṇ tōḻikkut talaimakaḷ colliyatu.]
   nīṭunilai araiya ceṅkuḻai iruppaik
       kōṭukaṭain taṉṉa koḷḷai vāṉpū
       āṭuparan taṉṉa īṉal eṇkiṉ
       3cēṭuciṉai yurīi uṇṭa miccil
       paiṅkuḻait taḻaiyar paḻaiyar makaḷir         5
       kaṇṭiraḷ nīḷamaik kaṭippiṟ ṟokuttuk
       kuṉṟakac ciṟukuṭi maṟukutoṟum maṟukumañ
       cīṟūr nāṭu palapiṟak koḻiyac
       ceṉṟōr aṉpilar tōḻi eṉṟum
       aruntuṟai muṟṟiya karuṅkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻp         10
       pāṇar ārppap palakalam utavi
       nāḷavaiyirunta naṉaimakiḻ titiyaṉ
       vēḷiroṭu 4porīiya kaḻitta
       vāḷvāy aṉṉa 5vaṟuñcuram iṟantē.
               ------ māmūlaṉār.

   -------
   (pāṭam) 3. tōṭu ciṉaiyuraii 4. porīik kaḻitta.
   5. aruñcuram.
   -------
   (co - ḷ.) 9. tōḻi-,
   1 - 8. nīṭu nilai araiya cemkuḻai iruppai - nīṇṭa nilaiyākiya
   aṭimarattiṉaiyuṭaiya civanta taḷirkaḷaiyuṭaiya iruppai maraṅkaḷiṉ, kōṭu
   kaṭaintaṉṉa koḷḷai vāṉ pū - tantattiṉaik kaṭaintāṟ pōṉṟa mikutiyāṉa
   veḷḷiya pūkkaḷil, āṭu paranta aṉṉa - āṭukaḷ parantāl otta, īṉal eṇkiṉ
   cēṭu - īṉṟa peṇ karaṭikaḷiṉ tiraṭci, ciṉai urīi uṇṭa miccil -
   kiḷaikaḷil parantu ceṉṟu uṇṭa miccilāyavaṟṟai, pai kuḻai taḻaiyar
   paḻaiyar makaḷir - paciya taḷirkaḷālāya taḻaiyuṭaiya rākiya eyiṉar
   makaḷir, kaṇtiraḷ nīḷ amaikkaṭippil tokuttu - kaṇukkaḷ tiraṇṭu nīṇṭa
   mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyil tiraṭṭi, kuṉṟakam ciṟu kuṭi maṟukutoṟum maṟukum - kuṉṟiṉ
   kaṇṇavākiya cīṟūriṉ terukkaṭōṟum cuḻaṉṟu viṟṟut tiriyum, amcīṟūr nāṭu
   pala piṟakku oḻiya - aḻakiya ciṟiya ūrkaḷaiyuṭaiya pala nāṭukaḷ piṉṉē
   kaḻiya,
   9 - 14. eṉṟum - eññāṉṟum, aru tuṟai muṟṟiya karukōṭṭu ciṟu yāḻp pāṇar
   ārppa - ariya icait tuṟaikaḷai muṟṟa uṇarnta kariya taṇṭiṉaiyuṭaiya
   ciṟiya yāḻiṉaiyuṭaiya pāṇarkaḷ āravāram ceyya, pala kalam utavi - pala
   aṇikalaṉkaḷai aḷittu, nāḷavai irunta naṉaimakiḻ titiyaṉ - nāḷōlakkam
   koṇṭirunta kaḷḷiṉ makiḻviṉai yuṭaiya titiyaṉ eṉpāṉ, vēḷiroṭu porīiya
   kaḻitta - vēḷiroṭu pōrceyaṟku uruviya, vāḷ vāy aṉṉa vaṟum curam iṟantu
   - vāḷiṉ vāyiṉai yotta koṭumaiyaiyuṭaiya vaṟaṇṭa curattiṉaik kaṭantu,
   9. ceṉṟōr aṉpu ilar - ceṉṟavarāya nam talaivar nampāl aṉpilātār āvar
   (eṉ ceyvām).
   (muṭipu) tōḻi! cīṟūr nāṭu pala piṟakku oḻiya, vaṟuñcuram iṟantu ceṉṟōr
   aṉpilar, eṉ ceyvām.
   (vi - rai.) iruppai vāṉ pū eṉka. āṭu parantaṉṉa eṉṟatu eṇkiṉ mikutiyai
   uṇarttiyapaṭi. cēṭu - tiraṭci, kūṭṭam eṉṟapaṭi. urīi - uruvi eṉṟumām.
   uṇṭa miccilākiya pūkkaḷai eṉka. paḻaiyar, kaḷ viṟpōr, eyiṉar. kaṭippu -
   mūṅkiṟ kuḻāyai uṇarttiṟṟu. maṟukum - tiriyum; viṟṟuttiriyum eṉṟapaṭi.
   aṉpilārāyp ceṉaṟār eṉṟumām. aruntuṟai - paṇṇum tiṟamum ākiya icaiyiṉ
   kūṟupāṭukaḷ. ārppa - makiḻcciyāl āravārikka. vāḷ vāy aṉṉa
   koṭumaiyuṭaiya eṉa viritturaikka.

   332. kuṟiñci
   [iravukkuṟikkaṇ ciṟaippuṟamākat tōḻikkut 1talaimakaḷ iyaṟpaṭa
   moḻintatu.]
   2muḷaivaḷar mutala mūṅkil murukkik
       kiḷaiyoṭu mēynta kēḻkiḷar yāṉai
       nīrnacai maruṅkiṉ 3niṟampārt toṭuṅkiya
       porumaraṇ uḻuvai tolaiccik kūrnuṉaik
       kurutic 4ceṅkōṭṭa ṭaḻituḷi kaḻāak         5
       5kaṉmukai aṭukkatatu meṉmela iyalic
       ceṟupakai 6vāṭṭiya cemmalo ṭaṟukāl
       yāḻicaip paṟavai imirap piṭipuṇarntu
       vāḻaiyañ cilampiṟ ṟuñcum nāṭaṉ
       niṉpurait takka cāyalaṉ eṉanī         10
       aṉpurait taṭaṅkak kūṟiya iṉcol
       vāyntaṉa vāḻi tōḻi vēṭṭōrkku
       amiḻtat taṉṉa kamaḻtār mārpiṉ
       vaṇṭiṭaip paṭāa muyakkamum
       taṇṭāk kātalum talai 7nāḷ pōṉmē.         15
               ---- kapilar.

   (pāṭam) 1. talaimakaḷ colliyatu. 2. muḻaivaḷar.
   3. nirampā toṭuṅkiya. 4. ceṅkōṭaḻi.
   5. kaṇ mukai. 6. ōṭṭiya cemmal.
   7. nāṭpōṉ.
